Silver rules backtest, 5 years: 20 Nov 2021 to 20 Nov 2026 (US Eastern time)

Every silver rule in the project (TA Section 35 and other TA/KD silver items, the 2026 document's silver section; your Sun notes are gold-only and are not used). Failure analysis reads the Moon, silver's classical significator (TA p.32; Kindle Ch15), and checks whether gold made the same move, recomputed with the Swiss-Ephemeris engine (Lahiri, mean node, whole-sign drishti) and scored against COMEX silver futures daily closes (Yahoo Finance SI=F continuous front month). Silver went from 24.8 (19 Nov 2021) to 41.0 (20 Nov 2026), +65.6%, peaking at 115.1 on 26 Jan 2026 (largest one-day fall -31.3%).

Rules checked (U2, U4 split by planet)57
Rules that fired49
Scored occurrences452
Worked201
Failed219
Flat (<0.5%)32
Hit rate48%
Rules: worked / mixed / failed13 / 17 / 16

Key findings

  1. Overall. 201 of 420 decided occurrences worked (48%), against 51% expected by chance from the base rates of those same occurrences (p = 0.875 for doing this well or better by luck). On the 104 trading days when more bearish than bullish silver rules were active, silver moved +0.47% on average over the next 5 days (60% up); on the 1025 bullish-majority days, +0.17% (51% up). Correlation of the daily net count with the next 5-day move: -0.059.
  2. Statistically clear results (p < 0.05 against the base rate):
    • None of the rules beat chance at that level.
  3. What worked (most decided occurrences first):
    • Venus-Mars or Sun-Mars together in any sign (Bullish): 19 of 30 worked (63% vs base 52%), average move +1.68% in the stated direction; p = 0.147; right in 4 of 5 years with a result
    • Mercury & Jupiter both retrograde (even in different signs) (Bullish): 4 of 6 worked (67% vs base 56%), average move +2.06% in the stated direction; p = 0.467; right in 3 of 5 years with a result
    • Venus alone in Leo or Libra (Bearish): 5 of 6 worked (83% vs base 45%), average move +0.87% in the stated direction; p = 0.070; right in 4 of 5 years with a result
    • Venus in Virgo with malefic (Bullish): 4 of 5 worked (80% vs base 55%), average move +3.76% in the stated direction; p = 0.257; right in 3 of 4 years with a result
    • Mars Vargottam in Cancer (Bearish): 3 of 4 worked (75% vs base 43%), average move +2.66% in the stated direction; p = 0.212; right in 3 of 4 years with a result
    • Sun & Moon together at Sankranti entering time (Bullish): 2 of 3 worked (67% vs base 51%), average move +2.32% in the stated direction; p = 0.519; right in 1 of 2 years with a result
    • Jupiter does Vedha of Venus (Bullish): 2 of 2 worked (100% vs base 51%), average move +1.68% in the stated direction; p = 0.261; right in 2 of 2 years with a result
    • Rahu in Dhanishta (market and bullion risk) (Bearish): 1 of 1 worked (100% vs base 84%), average move +28.75% in the stated direction; p = 0.837; right in 1 of 1 years with a result
    • Rahu does Vedha of Pushya (Bullish): 1 of 1 worked (100% vs base 16%), average move +15.48% in the stated direction; p = 0.160; right in 1 of 1 years with a result
    • Saturn-Neptune conjunction (Bullish): 1 of 1 worked (100% vs base 48%), average move +12.16% in the stated direction; p = 0.481; right in 1 of 1 years with a result
    • Sun eclipse in Falgun or Jyeshtha on Tuesday (Bullish): 1 of 1 worked (100% vs base 48%), average move +11.14% in the stated direction; p = 0.481; right in 1 of 1 years with a result
    • Chaitra Shukla Pratipada on Wednesday (Bullish): 1 of 1 worked (100% vs base 53%), average move +2.31% in the stated direction; p = 0.529; right in 1 of 1 years with a result
  4. What failed:
    • Mercury turns direct from retrograde (Bullish): 6 of 15 worked (40% vs base 54%), average move -0.16% in the stated direction; p = 0.905; right in 1 of 5 years with a result
    • Venus in Leo or Libra (Bearish): 3 of 11 worked (27% vs base 43%), average move -2.56% in the stated direction; p = 0.914; right in 1 of 5 years with a result
    • Mercury combust and retrograde does mutual Vedha with Moon (Bullish): 2 of 9 worked (22% vs base 51%), average move -2.28% in the stated direction; p = 0.984; right in 1 of 4 years with a result
    • Sun in Pushya or Dhanishta without benefic planets (Bullish): 2 of 8 worked (25% vs base 54%), average move -0.77% in the stated direction; p = 0.978; right in 1 of 5 years with a result
    • Mercury alone in Aries or Virgo (Bearish): 1 of 7 worked (14% vs base 47%), average move -0.90% in the stated direction; p = 0.989; right in 1 of 5 years with a result
    • Saturn direct from retrograde (Bearish): 1 of 4 worked (25% vs base 49%), average move -1.26% in the stated direction; p = 0.932; right in 1 of 4 years with a result
    • Venus combust in West (Bullish): 0 of 4 worked (0% vs base 50%), average move -4.66% in the stated direction; p = 1.000; right in 0 of 4 years with a result
    • Jupiter turns direct from retrograde (Bullish): 1 of 3 worked (33% vs base 52%), average move -1.95% in the stated direction; p = 0.891; right in 1 of 3 years with a result
    • Solar eclipse on a Wednesday (Bullish): 0 of 3 worked (0% vs base 52%), average move -2.23% in the stated direction; p = 1.000; right in 0 of 3 years with a result
    • Moon eclipse on Sunday (Bearish): 0 of 3 worked (0% vs base 44%), average move -2.51% in the stated direction; p = 1.000; right in 0 of 3 years with a result
    • Sun & Venus alone in Cancer (Bullish): 1 of 3 worked (33% vs base 51%), average move -3.00% in the stated direction; p = 0.882; right in 1 of 3 years with a result
    • Mars in Aquarius (Bullish): 1 of 3 worked (33% vs base 52%), average move -3.45% in the stated direction; p = 0.887; right in 1 of 3 years with a result
    • Mars in 4th pada of Ashlesha (Bearish): 0 of 2 worked (0% vs base 46%), average move -3.07% in the stated direction; p = 1.000; right in 0 of 2 years with a result
    • Sun in Leo with Mars or Jupiter retrograde (Bullish): 0 of 2 worked (0% vs base 52%), average move -4.20% in the stated direction; p = 1.000; right in 0 of 2 years with a result
  5. Why bullish rules failed. Conditions clearly more common when they failed: Gold moved against the rule too (>0.5%) (66% of failures vs 10% of successes); Mercury or Venus retrograde at the start (42% of failures vs 30% of successes); Jupiter sharing a sign with Rahu / Ketu / Mars / Saturn (28% of failures vs 18% of successes). More common when they worked: Waxing Moon (Shukla) at the start (45% of failures vs 63% of successes); A planet stations inside the interval (46% of failures vs 57% of successes).
  6. Why bearish rules failed. Conditions clearly more common when they failed: Gold moved against the rule too (>0.5%) (77% of failures vs 18% of successes); Waxing Moon (Shukla) at the start (55% of failures vs 33% of successes); Saturn / Rahu / Ketu on the Moon at the start (60% of failures vs 43% of successes); Eclipse inside the interval (30% of failures vs 19% of successes). More common when they worked: Mercury or Venus retrograde at the start (13% of failures vs 30% of successes); Opposite-tone rules outnumbered supporting ones (77% of failures vs 88% of successes).
  7. Silver follows gold. Over the same dates silver moved the same way as gold in 302 of 420 decided occurrences (72%). When gold moved against a silver rule the rule failed 155 of 180 times (86%); when gold moved the rule's way it worked 147 of 176 times (84%). So a silver rule's outcome was decided mostly by gold's direction: the rules add little that is specific to silver.
  8. Biggest single miss: late January 2026 (2026-01-26 → 2026-02-02, -33.3%, including the −31% day on 30 Jan, the same day gold fell 10.8%). Silver rules active: 3 bullish, 1 bearish. Sky: Sun in Shravana (lord Moon), Capricorn; Mercury with Sun, Venus with Sun, Mars with Sun; Jupiter Gemini Punarvasu R; 29 Jan: Mercury conjunction Venus in Capricorn; 31 Jan-01 Feb: Venus combust.
  9. The 15 biggest 5-day falls: bearish rules outnumbered bullish ones in 0 of them; in the rest the rule set leaned bullish going into the fall (it leans bullish on 82% of all trading days, so a bullish majority by itself carries little information). 2026-01-26 -33.3% (3:1); 2026-06-17 -17.5% (3:0); 2026-11-13 -16.3% (4:3); 2026-03-12 -16.3% (8:1); 2025-03-28 -16.0% (3:3); 2026-05-13 -14.7% (3:1); 2026-06-02 -13.6% (1:0); 2022-10-07 -10.8% (4:2).
  10. The 15 biggest 5-day rises: bullish rules outnumbered bearish ones in 13 of them.
  11. How far to trust this. 5 years give the frequent rules (vedha, Mercury/Sun contacts, paksha length, retrogrades) enough occurrences to judge; 25 rules still have fewer than 5 decided occurrences, and their records stay anecdotal. A rule that is right in most individual years is more trustworthy than one whose total comes from a single year; check the per-year columns.

How each occurrence was scored

  • Entry = the last daily close before the event (events at or after 17:00 ET count from the next session; panchang day-rules from that day's open).
  • Windows of 3 days or more (a planet in a nakshatra or sign, Sun contacts…) are scored from entry to the last close inside the window, clipped to the backtest period. Instants and short windows (new moon, darshan, rise/set, ingress, vedha starts) are scored on the 5-trading-day move; 1-, 3- and 10-day moves are shown as a sensitivity check.
  • Moves under ±0.5% are FLAT. Rules whose verdict is Mixed or Neutral (both vedha kinds at once; the Indian festival-demand dates) are listed but not scored.
  • Hit rate = worked ÷ (worked + failed); flat occurrences are left out of both.
  • Base rate = the hit rate a rule would get by chance: among all windows of the same length in the same backtest year that moved more than 0.5%, the share that moved the rule's way. Over the whole period a 5-day window that moved went up 54% of the time, so a bullish rule needs to beat 54% and a bearish rule 46% to show any edge. Using each year's own base rate means a bullish rule gets no credit just for firing in a rising year.
  • Rule verdict: Worked = hit rate at least 10 points above its base rate and the average move went the stated way; Failed = at least 10 points below and the average move went the wrong way; otherwise Mixed. p-value = the chance of doing at least this well by luck at the base rate (one-sided binomial); green if below 0.05, red if the rule did significantly worse than chance. The per-year columns show worked/decided in each Nov–Nov year, so a rule that only worked in one year stands out.

U2 and U4 are scored above per contact planet (U2 – Venus, U2 – Mercury, U2 – Jupiter, U2 – waxing Moon; U4 – Saturn, U4 – Rahu), because the whole rule's window restarts each time the set of contacts changes. Here every Sun-state stretch is scored by its rise-minus-fall count across U1–U4 (the Moon left out, as in the gold report's timeline; stretches with equal counts are not scored).

Do the rules add up? Net signal each day vs the next move

Each trading day: (number of distinct bullish silver rules active) − (number of distinct bearish silver rules active), with instants counted as active for one week; the combined Sun model is left out so nothing is counted twice. Correlation of that net count with the next day's return: -0.007; with the next 5 days: -0.059 (0 = no relation, positive = the rules pointed the right way).

Why rules failed: what keeps showing up

Which sky factors were present in the biggest moves?

Each factor is tested at the middle of each 5-day move. "All windows" is how often the factor was present across every 5-day window of the period; a factor only says something if it shows up clearly more often in the falls than in the rises (or the reverse) and more often than its all-period share. 15 moves each way is a small sample; treat this as a lead to examine, not a rule.
